Can we avoid solving LP each iteration by analytic q? The cross-LP is a linear program with interval matrix. We can solve it more efficiently myself, avoiding HIGHS overhead, perhaps allowing many iterations and larger n. This interval LP may have a specialized greedy algorithm!

Cross-LP:
min T s.t. for k=0..2n-2: ∑_{i in I_k} p_i q_i ≤ T, ∑q=1, q≥0, where I_k = indices i with 0≤k-i<n (i.e., i in [max(0,k-n+1), min(k,n-1)]).
Weights p_i fixed. This is minimizing maximum weighted coverage of intervals I_k by a probability q. Equivalently find q distribution over points 0..n-1 so that every interval I_k with universal target? Actually I_k are all intervals of the form [0..k] (k<n) and [k-n+1..n-1] (k≥n) and full [0..n-1] in middle. The middle interval includes all points, constraint T ≥ ∑p_i q_i (dot p,q). The left constraints for k=0..n-1: weighted sum over prefix [0..k] ≤T. Right symmetric: weighted sum over suffix [k-n+1..n-1] ≤T. Since p_i may not symmetric. So only 2n-1 constraints: prefixes, suffixes, and full. That's much smaller structure! Actually I_k: For k<n, i=0..k. For k>=n, i=k-n+1..n-1. For k=n-1, i=0..n-1. So left prefixes L_k = ∑_{i≤k} p_i q_i, right suffixes R_k = ∑_{i≥k-n+1} p_i q_i, and middle full D=∑ p_i q_i. We minimize max(max prefix, max suffix, D). This is a much simpler LP! We can solve greedily maybe. Because constraints only prefix/suffix weighted sums. Nice.

So cross-LP variables q_i≥0 sum1; minimize max( max_{0≤k<n} ∑_{i≤k} p_i q_i, max_{k<n} ∑_{i≥k?} p_i q_i? Let's index: for k=n-1+s, s=0..n-1, I = [s, n-1], weighted sum suffix starting s. Include full s=0. So constraints: all prefix sums of r_i = p_i q_i up to T; suffix sums of r_i up to T; total sum 1 (but r_i sum = p_i q_i, not 1! Wait D = ∑ p_i q_i, not ∑q. Primal (P): min T
s.t. A q ≤ T e (r constraints)
 1^T q =1
 q≥0.
Let y≥0 for A q - T e ≤0, γ free equality.
Lagrangian = T + y^T(Aq - T e) + γ(1^T q -1)
= T(1 - e^T y) + q^T(A^T y + γ 1) - γ.
Inf over q≥0 finite if A^T y + γ 1 ≥0; if e^T y=1; value -γ. Thus dual (D): maximize -γ subject to e^T y=1, A^T y + γ 1 ≥0, y≥0. Equivalently minimize γ subject to e^T y=1, A^T y + γ 1 ≥0, y≥0. Convolution utilities:
```
def _conv_full(a,b):
    n=a.size; m=b.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n*m <= 1000000: return np.convolve(a,b)
    size=1
    while size < n+m-1: size*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:n+m-1]
    return np.maximum(out,0)
```
For single a*a use `_convolve(a)`.
Need ensure sum normalized. Clipping small negatives.

Scoring:
`_score(p) = 2*p.size*max(conv_full(p,p))`. Since p sum1.
`_normalize(a)` clean.

Active set:
```
def _active_set(c, top_k=None, rel_tol=0.02):
    M=np.max(c)
    if top_k is None: mask=(c >= M*(1-rel_tol))
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
       mask zeros; mask[idx]=True
    return np.nonzero(mask)[0]
```
But if M small, use absolute `M - rel*M`. For active LP, we want top coefficients. We can use a list of top_k values: [1,2,3,5,8,15,30,60,120,250,500]. Also maybe all if n<=1024? For full 3809, top 250 enough.

```
   if R>=L:

      stop = k-L+1
```

Detailed `_solve_active_direction`:
```
def _solve_active_direction(p, active, M, reg_eps=0.0):
    n=p.size; r=active.size
    if r==0 or n==0: return None
    A = np.zeros((r, n+1), dtype=np.float64)
    for ii,k in enumerate(active):
       k=int(k)
       L = k - n + 1
       if L < 0: L=0
       R = k
       if R > n-1: R=n-1
       if R >= L:
          start = k - R
          stop = k - L + 1
          A[ii, L:R+1] = p[start:stop][::-1]
       A[ii, n] = -1.0
    cobj = np.zeros(n+1)
    cobj[n] = 1.0
    if reg_eps:
       cobj[:n] -= reg_eps * p  # minimize T - eps p·q
    Aeq = np.ones((1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    beq = np.array([1.0])
    lb = np.zeros(n+1); ub = np.full(n+1, np.inf)
    b_ub = np.full(r, max(M,1e-300)*(1+1e-8)+1e-12)
    try:
       res = linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, bounds=(lb,ub), method='highs')
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res is None or not res.success:
       return None
    q = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.clip(q,0,None)
    qs=sum; if qs<=0 return None
    q/=qs
    return q
```
Potential HIGHS with equality `sum q=1` and constraints many. If `reg_eps*p` where p sum1, objective q coefficients negative small. Fine.
Memory A for r=500,n=3810: 1.9M, ok.

- For active set, choose q uniform over indices minimizing the maximum active row coefficients? Could simply q = normalized p with mass to global minima: q uniform over indices with p_i <= min+10%. Or q = p. Actually fallback no improvement.

Main propose detailed timeline (functions top-level):
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2360):
```

```
def _coordinate_update_m(p,c,M,i,grid,refine_count):
```

Let's write helper functions carefully.

`_clean_array`:
```
def _clean_array(arr):
    try:
       a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if a.size==0: return None
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0,out=a)
    return a
```
If input has negative, clip 0.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=_clean_array(a)
    if a is None: return np.ones(1)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
    a=a/s
    # ensure tiny negatives 0
    np.clip(a,0,None,out=a)
    s2=a.sum()
    if s2>0: a/=s2
    return a
```

`_conv`:
```
def _conv(a,b):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size; m=b.size
    if n==0 or m==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n<m: maybe direct choice based on n*m
    if n*m <= 2_000_000:
        out=np.convolve(a,b)
    else:
        need=n+m-1
        size=1
        while size<need: size<<=1
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
        fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
        out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:need]
    return np.maximum(out,0.0)
```
Accuracy of FFT maybe small negative clipped. For final external internal selection, approximate. Could use direct for n<=2048 to ensure. For 3809 direct maybe 14M; FFT faster. Good.

`_self_conv(a): return _conv(a,a)`.

`_score_m(p,c=None): if c None conv; return 2*p.size*max`.

`_score_array(a): p=norm; c=self_conv; return 2n max`.

`_line_search_segment(c_p,cross,c_q)`:
Define top-level helper `_segment_vals(A,B,C,ts)` and `_best_t_segment`.
```
def _segment_vals(A,B,C,ts):
    ts=np.asarray(ts,dtype=np.float64)
    if ts.size==0: return np.empty(0)
    # chunk to avoid huge memory? We'll chunk 200.
    vals=np.empty(ts.size)
    chunk=200
    for s in range(0,ts.size,chunk):
       t=ts[s:s+chunk]
       vals[s:s+chunk]=np.max(A[None,:]+B[None,:]*t[:,None]+C[None,:]*(t[:,None]**2), axis=1)
    return vals
```
`_best_t_segment`:
```
def _best_t_segment(c_p,cross,c_q):
    A=c_p; B=2*(cross-c_p); C=c_q+c_p-2*cross
    ts1=np.linspace(0,1,81)
    ts2=np.logspace(-6,0,61)
    ts3=np.linspace(0,0.2,101)
    ts=np.unique(np.concatenate((ts1,ts2,ts3)))
    vals=_segment_vals(A,B,C,ts)
    idx=int(np.argmin(vals)); best_t=float(ts[idx]); best_val=float(vals[idx])
    # local around best
    if best_t <= 0.05:
       lo=0.0; hi=max(0.1,best_t*2+0.02)
    elif best_t >=0.95:
       lo=min(0.9,best_t-0.05); hi=1.0
    else:
       lo=max(0,best_t-0.05); hi=min(1,best_t+0.05)
    if hi>lo:
       tsl=np.linspace(lo,hi,161)
       vals2=_segment_vals(A,B,C,tsl)
       idx2=... update
    # optionally if best_val >= A.max()-? no.
    return best_t,best_val
```

```
def _local_optimize_m(p, duration, rng, use_coord=True):
    start0=time.monotonic(); deadline=start0+duration
    p=_normalize(p); c=_self_conv(p); M=float(c.max()); best=...
    # choose lp_deadline leave coordinate time based on n
    coord_budget = 0.0
    if p.size <= 1024: coord_budget = min(60, duration*0.35)
    elif p.size <= 2500: coord_budget = min(90, duration*0.25)
    else: coord_budget = min(120, duration*0.18)
    coord_deadline = max(start0+10, deadline - coord_budget)
    # But if duration short, no coord.
    topk_list = [1,3,8,20,50,120,300,700]
    c_len = 2*p.size-1
    topk_list = [max(1,min(t,c_len)) for t in topk_list]
    mode_idx=0; stag=0; perts=0; last_improve_t=start0
    while time.monotonic() < coord_deadline - 2:
        improved=False
        modes = topk_list[mode_idx: mode_idx+2]
        # sometimes include a larger random mode
        best_dir=None; best_newM=inf; best_t=0
        for tk in modes:
            if time > coord_deadline-1: break
            active=_active_indices(c, tk, rng, random_inject=0 if tk<=8 else 5)
            q=_solve_active_direction(p,active,M)
            if q is None:
                if tk<=3: q=_fallback_direction(p,active,M)
            if q is None: continue
            if np.linalg.norm(q-p) < 1e-8: continue
            cross=_conv(p,q); c_q=_self_conv(q)
            t,val=_best_t_segment(c,cross,c_q)
            if val < best_newM and val < M*(1-1e-10):
                best_newM=val; best_t=t; best_dir=q
        if best_dir is not None and np.isfinite(best_newM) and best_newM < M - 1e-12:
            p_new=(1-best_t)*p+best_t*best_dir
            p_new=_normalize(p_new)
            c_new=(1-best_t)*(1-best_t)*c + 2*best_t*(1-best_t)*cross? Need cross/c_q stored? We only stored best_dir but not cross/c_q. Could recompute p_new and scroll to avoid needing. Use p_new conv direct/FFT; cheap.
            p=c... M...
            if M < best_M: update
            improved=True; mode_idx=min(len(topk_list)-1, mode_idx) # stay
        else:
            if mode_idx < len(topk_list)-1: mode_idx +=1
            # fallback min support
            q=_fallback_direction(p,active? ) maybe active current? Need active from last? We can compute active top1.
```

Now coordinate function details. Let's write top-level `_coordinate_update_m(p,c,M,i,grid,refine_count)`:
Need `grid` positive values not including old/0? We'll include.
Pseudo:
```
def _coordinate_update_m(p,c,M,i,grid,refine_count):
    n=int(p.size); old=float(p[i])
    if not finite old or old<0: old=0
    D=1-old
    if D<0: D=0
    ks=slice(i, i+n)
    c_k=c[ks]
    coeff=2.0*p.copy()
    const=-2.0*old*p.copy()
    mid=i
    if 0<=mid<n:
        coeff[mid]=0.0
        const[mid]=-old*old
    max_unaff=0.0
    if i>0:
        mu=float(np.max(c[:i])); if finite ...
    end=i+n
    if end < c.size: ...
    xs=np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), grid, np.array([old])))
    xs=np.unique(xs[np.isfinite(xs)&(xs>=0)&(xs<=1e4)])
    if xs.size==0: return p,c,M,False
    aff = c_k[None,:] + coeff[None,:]*xs[:,None] + const[None,:]
    aff[:,mid] += xs*xs
    max_aff = np.max(aff,axis=1)
    maxc = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S=D+xs
    vals=np.full(xs.size,np.inf)
    valid=S>1e-15
    vals[valid]=maxc[valid]/(S[valid]*S[valid])
    idx=int(np.argmin(vals)); best_x=float(xs[idx]); best_m=float(vals[idx])
    tol=1e-12*max(1.0,M)
    if not finite best_m or best_m >= M - tol:
        return p,c,M,False
    if refine_count>0 and xs.size>1:
       left = 0.0 if idx==0 else float(xs[idx-1])
       right = float(xs[idx])*10.0 if idx==xs.size-1 else float(xs[idx+1])
       if best_x > 0:
          local=best_x*np.logspace(-1,1,int(refine_count))
       else:
          local=np.linspace(0,right,int(refine_count))
       local=np.unique(np.concatenate((local,[left,best_x,right])))
       ... evaluate, update best
    if best_m >= M - tol: return
    S=D+best_x; S2=S*S
    p_new=p/S
    p_new[i]=best_x/S
    c_new=c/S2
    aff_best = c_k + coeff*best_x + const
    aff_best[mid] += best_x*best_x
    c_new[ks]=aff_best/S2
    np.clip(p_new,0,None,p_new); np.clip(c_new,0,None,c_new)
    snew=p_new.sum()
    if snew<=0 or notfinite: return
    p_new/=snew; c_new/= (snew*snew)
    Mnew=float(c_new.max())
    if not finite or Mnew >= M - tol: return
    return p_new,c_new,Mnew,True
```

`_coordinate_optimize_m`:
```
def _coordinate_optimize_m(p,c,M,deadline,rng,grid,refine,max_perturb=3):
    p=c=...
    best...
    stag=0; perts=0; no_improve_cycles=0
    noise=[0.4,0.2,0.1]
    while time < deadline-2:
       n=p.size
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       improved=False
       for i in order:
           if time > deadline-1: break
           p,c,M,imp=_coordinate_update_m(p,c,M,int(i),grid,refine)
           if imp: improved=True
       # recompute to clean
       s=p.sum(); if s>0: p/p...; c=_self_conv(p); M=max; 
       if M < best_M - 1e-13: update best
       if improved:
           stag=0; no_improve=0
       else:
           stag+=1
           if stag>=2:
              if perts<max_perturb:
                  scale=noise[min(perts,len-1)]
                  p2=p*np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,n)); p2=normalize; c2=self_conv; M2=max
                  if M2 < M: p,c,M=p...
                  perts+=1; stag=0
              else:
                  break
       if time > deadline-6: break
    return best_p,best_c,best_M
```
This coordinate uses current M objective, not score. Good.

But this loop O(active*n) okay for fallback. If active None, use p min. We'll implement a bit:
```
def _fallback_direction(p, active):
  n=p.size
  if active is None or len(active)==0:
     minp=p.min(); mask=p <= minp + 1e-10 + 0.1*max(minp,1e-12)
  else:
     # cap active to 50
     caps=active[:50]
     minvals=np.zeros(n)
     for k in caps:
       L=max(0,k-n+1); R=min(k,n-1); temp=np.full(n,np.inf)
       if R>=L: temp[L:R+1]=p[k-R:k-L+1][::-1]
       minvals=np.minimum(minvals,temp)
     minv=np.min(minvals[np.isfinite(minvals)])
     mask = minvals <= minv + max(1e-10, 0.1*max(minv,1e-10))
  if none: q uniform over argmin p
  q=mask.astype(float); qs=sum; if qs==0 q uniform; q/=qs
```
Good.

Perturb:
```
def _perturb_p(p, rng, scale):
   n=p.size
   p2=p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,scale,n))
   p2 += 1e-12
   return _normalize(p2)
```

`_local_optimize_m` detailed code:
```
def _local_optimize_m(p, duration, rng, use_coord=True):
    p=_normalize(p)
    c=_self_conv(p)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(1.0,float(duration))
    n=p.size
    if n<=1: return p,M
    if duration < 20:
       return _coordinate...? Maybe just return.
    coord_budget = 0.0
    if use_coord and duration >= 90:
       if n <= 256: coord_budget = min(30, duration*0.35)
       elif n <= 1024: coord_budget = min(60, duration*0.30)
       elif n <= 2500: coord_budget = min(90, duration*0.22)
       else: coord_budget = min(120, duration*0.16)
    lp_deadline = max(start+5, deadline - coord_budget)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_M=M
    c_len=2*n-1
    if n <= 128: topk_list=[1,3,8,20,50,min(80,c_len)]
    elif n <= 512: topk_list=[1,3,8,20,60,150,min(300,c_len)]
    else: topk_list=[1,3,8,20,60,150,min(350,c_len)]
    # unique sorted
    topk_list = sorted(set(max(1,min(t,c_len)) for t in topk_list))
    mode_idx=0; stag=0; perts=0
    FLOOR=1e-9
    while time.monotonic() < lp_deadline - 2:
```

```
def _random_blocks(n,K,rng):
  n=max(1,int(n)); K=max(1,int(K))
  lens=rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.0))))*n
  raw=np.floor(lens).astype(int); rem=n-raw.sum()
  # add rem to largest fractional
  frac=np.argsort(lens - raw)
  for j in range(rem): raw[frac[(K-j-1)]] +=1
  # ensure positive
  masses=rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, float(rng.uniform(0.3,2.0))))
  out=np.zeros(n); idx=0
  for i,L in enumerate(raw):
    if L>0: out[idx:idx+L]=masses[i]/L
    idx+=L
  return _normalize(out)
```
If raw some zero? Dirichlet positive floor may zero for very small; set zeros mass 0, okay. Use `_normalize`.

Let's code `_local_optimize_m` more cleanly:
```
def _local_optimize_m(p, duration, rng, use_coord=True):
    p = _normalize(p)
    c = _self_conv(p)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else float('inf')
    start = time.monotonic()
    if duration <= 5.0 or M != M:
        return p, M
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(duration))
    n = p.size
```

```python
"""
Search heuristic for minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative coefficient sequences.

The implementation combines:
  * fast FFT/direct convolution scoring,
  * prototype generation and block expansion,
  * active-set LP directions (closely related to the previous SOTA LP,
    but restricted to near-maximal convolution coefficients and with
    added spread constraints to obtain larger useful line-search steps),
  * exact 1-D coordinate polishing.

It returns the best valid list found within the supplied time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _candidate_key(cand):
    return cand[0]


def _ext_key(tup):
    return tup[0]


def _clean_array(arr):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    return a


def _normalize(a):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    np.clip(a, 0.0, None, out=a)
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s2


def _conv(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(b.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n * m <= 2_000_000:
        out = np.convolve(a, b)
    else:
        need = n + m - 1
        size = 1
        while size < need:
            size <<= 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
        out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)[:need]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _self_conv(a):
    return _conv(a, a)


def _score_array(a):
    p = _normalize(a)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = _self_conv(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * p.size * np.max(c))


def _eval_external(seq):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
        v = evaluate_sequence(vals)
        return float(v)
    except Exception:
        return _score_array(seq)


def _add_candidate(candidates, seq, score, limit=40):
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    p = _normalize(seq).copy()
    candidates.append([float(score), int(p.size), p])
    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    while len(candidates) > limit:
        candidates.pop()


def _two_level(n, mix=2.0 / 3.0):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = n // 2
    rest = n - m
    if m > 0:
        p[:m] = float(mix) / float(m)
    if rest > 0:
        p[m:] = float(1.0 - mix) / float(rest)
    return _normalize(p)


def _paired_initial(n, rng, alpha_w=1.0, alpha_split=0.3):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = (n + 1) // 2
    weights = rng.dirichlet(np.full(m, float(alpha_w), dtype=np.float64))
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    split_alpha = max(0.01, float(alpha_split))
    for r in range(m):
        a = r
        b = n - 1 - r
        if a == b:
            p[a] = weights[r]
        else:
            if rng.random() < 0.70:
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    q0, q1 = 1.0, 0.0
                else:
                    q0, q1 = 0.0, 1.0
            else:
                q = rng.dirichlet(np.full(2, split_alpha, dtype=np.float64))
                q0, q1 = float(q[0]), float(q[1])
            p[a] = weights[r] * q0
            p[b] = weights[r] * q1
    return _normalize(p)


def _random_blocks(n, K, rng):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    K = max(1, min(int(K), n))
    raw_len = rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, float(rng.uniform(0.25, 2.0)), dtype=np.float64)) * float(n)
    lengths = np.floor(raw_len).astype(np.int64)
    rem = int(n - int(np.sum(lengths)))
    if rem > 0:
        order = np.argsort(raw_len - lengths)
        for j in range(rem):
            idx = int(order[-1 - j])
            if 0 <= idx < K:
                lengths[idx] += 1
    masses = rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, float(rng.uniform(0.25, 2.0)), dtype=np.float64))
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = 0
    for i in range(K):
        L = int(lengths[i])
        if L > 0:
            out[pos:pos + L] = float(masses[i]) / float(L)
            pos += L
    return _normalize(out)


def _make_initial(n, strategy, rng):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    st = int(strategy) % 14
    if st == 0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 1:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 2:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, 0.35, dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 3:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, 0.12, dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 4:
        p = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
    elif st == 5:
        p = _paired_initial(n, rng, 0.6, 0.2)
    elif st == 6:
        p = _paired_initial(n, rng, 1.2, 0.8)
    elif st == 7:
        p = _two_level(n, float(rng.uniform(0.55, 0.85)))
    elif st == 8:
        p = _random_blocks(n, 2, rng)
    elif st == 9:
        p = _random_blocks(n, 4, rng)
    elif st == 10:
        p = _random_blocks(n, 8, rng)
    elif st == 11:
        p = _random_blocks(n, 16, rng)
    elif st == 12:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x / n + phase) + 0.05 * rng.random(n)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    else:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.cos(np.pi * x / n + phase) + 0.05 * rng.random(n)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    return _normalize(p)


def _expand_to_length(proto, target_len):
    target_len = int(target_len)
    p = _normalize(proto)
    R = int(p.size)
    if R <= 0 or target_len <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, target_len), dtype=np.float64)
    if R == target_len:
        return p.copy()
    if R > target_len:
        out = np.ones(target_len, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(target_len):
            lo = (j * R) // target_len
            hi = max(lo + 1, ((j + 1) * R) // target_len)
            chunk = p[lo:hi]
            out[j] = float(np.mean(chunk)) if chunk.size > 0 else 1.0
        return _normalize(out)
    base = target_len // R
    rem = target_len % R
    lengths = np.full(R, base, dtype=np.int64)
    if rem > 0:
        lengths[:rem] += 1
    out = np.zeros(target_len, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = 0
    for i in range(R):
        L = int(lengths[i])
        if L > 0:
            out[idx:idx + L] = float(p[i]) / float(L)
            idx += L
    return _normalize(out)


def _resample_linear(proto, target_len):
    target_len = int(target_len)
    p = _normalize(proto)
    if p.size == 0 or target_len <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, target_len), dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 1:
        return np.full(max(1, target_len), float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, max(1, target_len), dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(xn, xp, p)
    return _normalize(out)


def _active_indices(c, topk, rng, random_inject=0):
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    k = int(topk)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    if k >= L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)
    ri = max(0, int(random_inject))
    if ri > 0:
        M = float(np.max(c))
        near = np.nonzero(c >= M * 0.85)[0]
        if near.size > 0:
            if ri < near.size:
                extra = rng.choice(near, size=ri, replace=False)
            else:
                extra = near
            idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((idx, extra)))
    return np.sort(idx).astype(np.int64)


def _solve_active_direction(p, active, M, reg_eps=1.0e-8, max_q=None):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    n = int(p.size)
    r = int(active.size)
    if n <= 0 or r <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((r, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for ii, kk in enumerate(active):
        k = int(kk)
        L = k - n + 1
        if L < 0:
            L = 0
        R = k
        if R > n - 1:
            R = n - 1
        if R >= L:
            start = k - R
            stop = k - L + 1
            A[ii, L:R + 1] = p[start:stop][::-1]
        A[ii, n] = -1.0

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0
    if reg_eps and reg_eps > 0.0:
        c_obj[:n] = -float(reg_eps) * np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)

    b_ub = np.full(r, float(M) * (1.0 + 1.0e-9) + 1.0e-15, dtype=np.float64)

    A_eq = np.ones((1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    lb = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    ub = np.full(n + 1, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    if max_q is not None:
        mq = float(max_q)
        if not np.isfinite(mq) or mq <= 0.0:
            mq = 1.0 / n
        if mq * n < 1.0:
            mq = 1.001 * (1.0 / n)
        mq = min(1.0, mq)
        ub[:n] = mq

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=(lb, ub),
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    q = np.asarray(x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    qs = float(np.sum(q))
    if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 1.0e-15:
        return None
    return q / qs


def _segment_vals(A, B, C, ts):
    ts = np.asarray(ts, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if ts.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.empty(ts.size, dtype=np.float64)
    chunk = 128
    for s in range(0, ts.size, chunk):
        t = ts[s:s + chunk].reshape(-1, 1)
        vals[s:s + chunk] = np.max(A[None, :] + B[None, :] * t + C[None, :] * (t * t), axis=1)
    return vals


def _best_t_segment(c_p, cross, c_q):
    A = c_p
    B = 2.0 * (cross - c_p)
    C = c_q + c_p - 2.0 * cross

    ts1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 81)
    ts2 = np.logspace(-6.0, 0.0, 61)
    ts3 = np.linspace(0.0, 0.25, 101)
    ts = np.unique(np.concatenate((ts1, ts2, ts3)))
    vals = _segment_vals(A, B, C, ts)
    idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_t = float(ts[idx])
    best_val = float(vals[idx])

    if best_t <= 0.05:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = min(1.0, max(0.10, best_t * 2.0 + 0.02))
    elif best_t >= 0.95:
        lo = max(0.0, min(0.92, best_t - 0.05))
        hi = 1.0
    else:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.05)
        hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.05)

    if hi > lo:
        ts_local = np.linspace(lo, hi, 161)
        vals_local = _segment_vals(A, B, C, ts_local)
        idx2 = int(np.argmin(vals_local))
        if float(vals_local[idx2]) < best_val:
            best_val = float(vals_local[idx2])
            best_t = float(ts_local[idx2])

    return best_t, best_val


def _fallback_direction(p, active=None):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if active is None or len(active) == 0:
        minp = float(np.min(p))
        tol = max(1.0e-12, 0.1 * max(minp, 1.0e-12))
        mask = p <= minp + tol
        if not np.any(mask):
            j = int(np.argmin(p))
            mask = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
            mask[j] = True
        q = mask.astype(np.float64)
    else:
        minvals = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in list(active)[:50]:
            k = int(kk)
            L = k - n + 1
            if L < 0:
                L = 0
            R = k
            if R > n - 1:
                R = n - 1
            temp = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
            if R >= L:
                start = k - R
                stop = k - L + 1
                temp[L:R + 1] = p[start:stop][::-1]
            minvals = np.minimum(minvals, temp)
        finite = np.isfinite(minvals)
        if not np.any(finite):
            return _normalize(p)
        minv = float(np.min(minvals[finite]))
        tol = max(1.0e-12, 0.1 * max(minv, 1.0e-12))
        mask = finite & (minvals <= minv + tol)
        if not np.any(mask):
            mask = finite.copy()
        q = mask.astype(np.float64)
    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return q / s


def _perturb_p(p, rng, scale):
    p = _normalize(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    p2 = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), n))
    p2 += 1.0e-12
    return _normalize(p2)


def _coord_grid_params(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 32:
        return 34, 6
    if n <= 128:
        return 24, 5
    if n <= 512:
        return 18, 4
    if n <= 2048:
        return 13, 3
    return 10, 3


def _coordinate_update_m(p, c, M, i, grid, refine_count):
    n = int(p.size)
    old = float(p[i])
    if not np.isfinite(old) or old < 0.0:
        old = 0.0
    D = 1.0 - old
    if D < 0.0:
        D = 0.0

    ks = slice(i, i + n)
    c_k = c[ks]

    coeff = 2.0 * p.copy()
    const = -2.0 * old * p.copy()
    mid = int(i)
    if 0 <= mid < n:
        coeff[mid] = 0.0
        const[mid] = -old * old

    max_unaff = 0.0
    if i > 0:
        mm = float(np.max(c[:i]))
        if np.isfinite(mm) and mm > max_unaff:
            max_unaff = mm
    end = i + n
    if end < c.size:
        mm = float(np.max(c[end:]))
        if np.isfinite(mm) and mm > max_unaff:
            max_unaff = mm

    xs = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64),
                         np.asarray(grid, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
                         np.array([old], dtype=np.float64)))
    xs = np.unique(xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= 1.0e4)])
    if xs.size == 0:
        return p, c, M, False

    aff = c_k[None, :] + coeff[None, :] * xs[:, None] + const[None, :]
    aff[:, mid] = aff[:, mid] + xs * xs
    max_aff = np.max(aff, axis=1)
    maxc = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S = D + xs
    vals = np.full(xs.size, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
    valid = S > 1.0e-15
    vals[valid] = maxc[valid] / (S[valid] * S[valid])
    idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_x = float(xs[idx])
    best_m = float(vals[idx])

    tol = 1.0e-12 * max(1.0, float(M))
    if not np.isfinite(best_m) or best_m >= M - tol:
        return p, c, M, False

    if refine_count > 0 and xs.size > 1:
        left = 0.0 if idx == 0 else float(xs[idx - 1])
        right = float(xs[idx]) * 10.0 if idx == xs.size - 1 else float(xs[idx + 1])
        if best_x > 0.0:
            local = best_x * np.logspace(-1.0, 1.0, int(refine_count))
        else:
            local = np.linspace(0.0, max(0.0, right), int(refine_count))
        local = np.concatenate((local, [left, best_x, right]))
        local = np.unique(local[np.isfinite(local) & (local >= 0.0) & (local <= 1.0e4)])
        if local.size > 0:
            aff2 = c_k[None, :] + coeff[None, :] * local[:, None] + const[None, :]
            aff2[:, mid] = aff2[:, mid] + local * local
            max_aff2 = np.max(aff2, axis=1)
            maxc2 = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff2)
            S2 = D + local
            vals2 = np.full(local.size, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
            valid2 = S2 > 1.0e-15
            vals2[valid2] = maxc2[valid2] / (S2[valid2] * S2[valid2])
            idx2 = int(np.argmin(vals2))
            if np.isfinite(vals2[idx2]) and float(vals2[idx2]) < best_m:
                best_m = float(vals2[idx2])
                best_x = float(local[idx2])

    if not np.isfinite(best_m) or best_m >= M - tol:
        return p, c, M, False

    S = D + best_x
    if S <= 1.0e-15:
        return p, c, M, False
    S2 = S * S

    p_new = p / S
    p_new[i] = best_x / S

    c_new = c / S2
    aff_best = c_k + coeff * best_x + const
    aff_best[mid] = aff_best[mid] + best_x * best_x
    c_new[ks] = aff_best / S2

    np.clip(p_new, 0.0, None, out=p_new)
    np.clip(c_new, 0.0, None, out=c_new)

    snew = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 0.0:
        return p, c, M, False
    p_new = p_new / snew
    c_new = c_new / (snew * snew)

    s2 = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        return p, c, M, False
    p_new = p_new / s2
    c_new = c_new / (s2 * s2)

    Mnew = float(np.max(c_new)) if c_new.size > 0 else float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(Mnew) or Mnew >= M - tol:
        return p, c, M, False
    return p_new, c_new, Mnew, True


def _coordinate_optimize_m(p, c, M, deadline, rng, grid, refine_count, max_perturb=3):
    p = _normalize(p).copy()
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    M = float(M)
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        c = _self_conv(p)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")

    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p, c, M

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_m = M

    stag = 0
    perts = 0
    noise = [0.35, 0.20, 0.10, 0.05]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        improved = False
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1.0:
                break
            p, c, M, imp = _coordinate_update_m(p, c, M, int(i), grid, refine_count)
            if imp:
                improved = True

        s = float(np.sum(p))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            break
        p = p / s
        c = _self_conv(p)
        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(M) and M < best_m - 1.0e-14:
            best_m = M
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
            stag = 0
        else:
            if improved:
                stag = 0
            else:
                stag += 1

        if not improved and stag >= 2:
            if perts < int(max_perturb):
                scale = noise[min(perts, len(noise) - 1)]
                p2 = _perturb_p(best_p, rng, scale)
                c2 = _self_conv(p2)
                m2 = float(np.max(c2)) if c2.size else float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
                    best_m = m2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    best_c = c2.copy()
                p = best_p.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                M = best_m
                perts += 1
                stag = 0
            else:
                break

    return best_p, best_c, float(best_m)


def _local_optimize_m(p, duration, rng, use_coord=True):
    p = _normalize(p)
    c = _self_conv(p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else float("inf")
    start = time.monotonic()

    if duration <= 5.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return p, M

    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(duration))
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p, M

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_m = M

    if duration < 80.0:
        coord_budget = 0.30 * duration if (use_coord and duration > 15.0) else 0.0
    else:
        if n <= 256:
            coord_budget = min(30.0, 0.35 * duration)
        elif n <= 1024:
            coord_budget = min(60.0, 0.30 * duration)
        elif n <= 2500:
            coord_budget = min(90.0, 0.22 * duration)
        else:
            coord_budget = min(120.0, 0.16 * duration)

    lp_deadline = max(start + 3.0, deadline - coord_budget)
    if linprog is None:
        lp_deadline = start + 0.75

    c_len = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 128:
        topk_list = [1, 3, 8, 20, 50, 80]
    elif n <= 512:
        topk_list = [1, 3, 8, 20, 60, 150, 300]
    else:
        topk_list = [1, 3, 8, 20, 60, 150, 320]
    topk_list = sorted(set(max(1, min(int(t), c_len)) for t in topk_list))
    if not topk_list:
        topk_list = [1]

    mode_idx = 0
    stag = 0
    perts = 0

    while time.monotonic() < lp_deadline - 2.0:
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M > best_m:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            M = float(best_m)

        si = mode_idx % len(topk_list)
        modes = [topk_list[(si + j) % len(topk_list)] for j in range(2)]
        maxp = float(np.max(p)) if n > 0 else 0.0
        mq1 = max(8.0 / n, 1.001 * max(maxp, 1.0 / n) + 1.0e-14)
        mq2 = max(20.0 / n, 1.001 * max(maxp, 1.0 / n) + 1.0e-14)

        best_new_m = float("inf")
        best_q = None
        best_cross = None
        best_cq = None
        best_t = 0.0

        for tk in modes:
            if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline - 3.0:
                break
            active = _active_indices(c, tk, rng, 3 if tk >= 20 else 0)
            for mq in (None, mq1, mq2):
                if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline - 2.0:
                    break
                q = _solve_active_direction(p, active, M, 1.0e-8, mq)
                if q is None:
                    continue
                if np.linalg.norm(q - p) < 1.0e-10:
                    continue
                cross = _conv(p, q)
                cq = _self_conv(q)
                t, val = _best_t_segment(c, cross, cq)
                if np.isfinite(val) and t > 0.0 and val < best_new_m and val < M - 1.0e-11:
                    best_new_m = float(val)
                    best_q = q
                    best_cross = cross
                    best_cq = cq
                    best_t = float(t)

        improved = False
        if (best_q is not None and np.isfinite(best_new_m)
                and best_t > 0.0 and best_new_m < M - 1.0e-11):
            p_try = (1.0 - best_t) * p + best_t * best_q
            p_try = _normalize(p_try)
            c_try = _self_conv(p_try)
            m_try = float(np.max(c_try)) if c_try.size else float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(m_try) and m_try < M - 1.0e-13:
                p = p_try.copy()
                c = c_try.copy()
                M = m_try
                improved = True
                if M < best_m:
                    best_m = M
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
                stag = 0
                mode_idx = si
        else:
            active = _active_indices(c, modes[0], rng, 0)
            q = _fallback_direction(p, active)
            if q is not None and np.linalg.norm(q - p) > 1.0e-10:
                cross = _conv(p, q)
                cq = _self_conv(q)
                t, val = _best_t_segment(c, cross, cq)
                if np.isfinite(val) and t > 0.0 and val < M - 1.0e-11:
                    p_try = (1.0 - t) * p + t * q
                    p_try = _normalize(p_try)
                    c_try = _self_conv(p_try)
                    m_try = float(np.max(c_try)) if c_try.size else float("inf")
                    if np.isfinite(m_try) and m_try < M - 1.0e-13:
                        p = p_try.copy()
                        c = c_try.copy()
                        M = m_try
                        improved = True
                        if M < best_m:
                            best_m = M
                            best_p = p.copy()
                            best_c = c.copy()

        if not improved:
            stag += 1
            if stag >= 3:
                scale = 0.35 / (1.0 + 0.5 * float(perts))
                p2 = _perturb_p(best_p, rng, scale)
                c2 = _self_conv(p2)
                m2 = float(np.max(c2)) if c2.size else float("inf")
                if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
                    best_m = m2
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    best_c = c2.copy()
                p = best_p.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                M = float(best_m)
                perts += 1
                stag = 0
                mode_idx = (mode_idx + 1) % len(topk_list)
            if perts >= 12:
                break

    if use_coord and duration > 10.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
        grid_count, refine_count = _coord_grid_params(n)
        grid = np.concatenate((np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64),
                               np.logspace(-10.0, 2.0, int(grid_count))))
        p2, c2, m2 = _coordinate_optimize_m(best_p, best_c, best_m, deadline, rng, grid, refine_count)
        if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
            best_m = m2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_c = c2.copy()

    return best_p, float(best_m)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0):
    """Search for a high-quality non-negative coefficient sequence."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    candidates = []

    # Existing state-of-art, if available.
    try:
        hseq = _clean_array(height_sequence_1)
        if hseq is not None:
            hs = _score_array(hseq)
            _add_candidate(candidates, hseq, hs)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Safe baselines.
    for L in (256, 1024, 3809):
        for mix in (0.50, 0.65, 0.78):
            base = _two_level(L, mix)
            bs = _score_array(base)
            _add_candidate(candidates, base, bs)

    # Random/structured prototypes.
    initial_plan = [
        (64, 2),
        (128, 3),
        (256, 3),
        (512, 3),
        (1024, 2),
        (2048, 2),
        (3809, 3),
    ]
    for n, trials in initial_plan:
        for _ in range(int(trials)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            st = int(rng.integers(0, 14))
            p = _make_initial(n, st, rng)
            s = _score_array(p)
            _add_candidate(candidates, p, s)

    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    print(f"initial best internal score: {candidates[0][0] if candidates else float('nan'):.10f} "
          f"len={candidates[0][1] if candidates else 0}", flush=True)

    # Phase 1: optimize short prototypes.
    short_budget = min(240.0, max(60.0, 0.16 * max(30.0, deadline - start)))
    short_pool = [c for c in candidates if c[1] <= 512]
    short_pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    short_cands = short_pool[:4]
    if short_cands:
        per_short = max(12.0, short_budget / float(max(1, len(short_cands))))
        for cand in short_cands:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 210.0:
                break
            seq, mbest = _local_optimize_m(cand[2], per_short, rng, use_coord=True)
            sc = 2.0 * int(seq.size) * float(mbest)
            _add_candidate(candidates, seq, sc)
        print(f"after short prototypes: best={candidates[0][0]:.10f} len={candidates[0][1]}", flush=True)

    # Expand the best short sequences to useful full lengths.
    short_pool = [c for c in candidates if c[1] <= 512]
    short_pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    for cand in short_pool[:3]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
            break
        for target in (1024, 2048, 3809):
            e1 = _expand_to_length(cand[2], target)
            s1 = _score_array(e1)
            _add_candidate(candidates, e1, s1)
            e2 = _resample_linear(cand[2], target)
            s2 = _score_array(e2)
            _add_candidate(candidates, e2, s2)

    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    # Ensure at least one length-3809 candidate.
    if not any(c[1] == 3809 for c in candidates):
        base = _two_level(3809)
        _add_candidate(candidates, base, _score_array(base))

    # Phase 2: optimize promising medium/full candidates.
    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    full_pool = [c for c in candidates if c[1] >= 1024]
    full_pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    if not full_pool:
        full_pool = [candidates[0]]

    remaining = max(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - 120.0)
    slices = [0.38, 0.28, 0.18, 0.12, 0.08]
    full_cands = full_pool[:5]
    for j, cand in enumerate(full_cands):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
            break
        dur = float(remaining) * float(slices[min(j, len(slices) - 1)])
        if time.monotonic() + dur > deadline - 75.0:
            dur = max(15.0, deadline - 75.0 - time.monotonic())
        if dur < 12.0:
            continue
        seq, mbest = _local_optimize_m(cand[2], dur, rng, use_coord=True)
        sc = 2.0 * int(seq.size) * float(mbest)
        _add_candidate(candidates, seq, sc)
        print(f"full iter {j}: score={sc:.10f} len={int(seq.size)} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s",
              flush=True)

        # If this is not length 3809, also push an expansion into the pool.
        if seq.size != 3809:
            for target in (2048, 3809):
                exp = _expand_to_length(seq, target)
                s = _score_array(exp)
                _add_candidate(candidates, exp, s)

    # Phase 3: if time remains, work on the current best candidate.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
        best = candidates[0]
        dur = max(30.0, (deadline - 120.0 - time.monotonic()))
        seq, mbest = _local_optimize_m(best[2], dur, rng, use_coord=True)
        sc = 2.0 * int(seq.size) * float(mbest)
        _add_candidate(candidates, seq, sc)
        print(f"final best candidate: score={sc:.10f} len={int(seq.size)}", flush=True)

    # Final authoritative check on a small set of candidates.
    candidates.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    final_pool = candidates[:12]

    # Always include the original SOTA and a safe 3809 baseline.
    try:
        hseq = _clean_array(height_sequence_1)
        if hseq is not None:
            _add_candidate(final_pool, hseq, _score_array(hseq), limit=20)
    except Exception:
        pass
    base3809 = _two_level(3809)
    _add_candidate(final_pool, base3809, _score_array(base3809), limit=20)
    final_pool.sort(key=_candidate_key)

    ext_evals = []
    for cand in final_pool[:10]:
        sc = _eval_external(cand[2])
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            ext_evals.append((float(sc), cand))
            print(f"external score={sc:.10f} len={cand[1]}", flush=True)

    if not ext_evals:
        chosen = _two_level(3809)
        return [float(x) for x in _normalize(chosen)]

    ext_evals.sort(key=_ext_key)
    chosen_score = ext_evals[0][0]
    chosen_seq = ext_evals[0][1][2]

    # Prefer length 3809 if it is almost as good.
    best3809 = None
    for sc, cand in ext_evals:
        if cand[1] == 3809:
            if best3809 is None or sc < best3809[0]:
                best3809 = (sc, cand[2])
    if best3809 is not None and best3809[0] <= chosen_score + 0.01:
        chosen_seq = best3809[1]
        chosen_score = best3809[0]

    out = _normalize(chosen_seq)
    print(f"chosen external score={chosen_score:.10f} len={out.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
